TL;DR: 本文提出BinMirror，将二进制反混淆从"结构变换"范式转变为"行为驱动合成"范式。通过将动态执行轨迹和交互快照作为行为规范，BinMirror将二进制反混淆重构为程序合成任务，在150万个混淆二进制上评估，极端混淆下单元测试Pass@1达74.5%，显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有二进制反混淆方法主要基于反编译+结构变换的范式，但编译过程丢失了高级语义（如精确类型信息和源码级结构），导致恢复代码质量低且缺乏行为等价性的保证。而源码级反混淆研究虽多，但实际场景常无源码可用，二进制级反混淆亟需探索。

Method: BinMirror的核心洞见是：虽然混淆扭曲了程序内部结构，但保持语义的变换必须保留可观察的执行行为。基于此，BinMirror将二进制反混淆重构为行为规范指导的程序合成任务（behavior-specification-guided program synthesis），以动态执行轨迹和交互快照作为行为规范，合成高质量源码，并通过运行时观测验证合成结果。

Result: 在150万个合成混淆二进制上的广泛评估表明，BinMirror显著优于最先进的基线方法，在极端混淆条件下实现了74.5%的单元测试Pass@1。

Conclusion: BinMirror通过范式转换（从结构变换到行为驱动合成）有效解决了二进制反混淆中代码质量低和行为保真不确定的问题，为真实世界安全分析恢复语义清晰度提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Deobfuscation is critical to reverse engineering and security analysis because it restores the readability and analyzability of obfuscated code. However, existing research primarily focuses on source-code deobfuscation, while binary-level deobfuscation remains largely underexplored despite its practical importance when source code is unavailable. Existing binary deobfuscation methods typically decompile binaries into pseudocode and then apply structural transformations. However, because compilation discards high-level semantics such as precise type information and source-level structures, this decompilation-based paradigm often produces low-quality code and provides limited assurance that the recovered code preserves the runtime behavior of the original program. To address these limitations, we propose a paradigm shift from structural transformation to behavior-driven synthesis. Our core insight is that although obfuscation distorts a program's internal structure, semantics-preserving transformations must retain its observable execution behavior. Based on this insight, we introduce BinMirror, an approach that reformulates binary deobfuscation as a behavior-specification-guided program synthesis task. By treating dynamic execution traces and interaction snapshots as behavioral specifications, BinMirror synthesizes high-quality source code and validates it against runtime observations collected from heavily obfuscated binaries. Extensive evaluations on 1.5 million synthetically obfuscated binaries show that BinMirror significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving a unit-test Pass@1 of 74.5% under extreme obfuscation. These results demonstrate the practical utility of BinMirror in restoring semantic clarity for real-world security analysis.

</details>


### [245] [Toward Understanding Operating System Defects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20643)
*Hongyao Zuo,Jiali Li,Jiajun Jiang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对来自三个代表性操作系统（Android、Linux、HarmonyOS）的1,500个缺陷进行了大规模实证研究，涵盖移动和桌面环境，从多个维度（缺陷发生层级、影响功能、触发方式、严重程度、修复涉及的代码元素）进行分析，并通过维度间联合分析和跨系统相似性比较，总结出全面的OS缺陷特征，为OS缺陷检测与调试提供可行的指导建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 操作系统是所有软件系统的基础，OS缺陷可能导致系统崩溃和数据损坏等严重后果，影响数十亿用户。理解OS缺陷的特征是保障OS质量的基础性工作，但目前缺乏大规模、跨系统的OS缺陷特征研究，因此需要开展系统性的缺陷分析以支持有效的缺陷检测和调试方法设计。

Method: 收集并分析了1,500个来自Android、Linux和HarmonyOS三个不同且具有代表性的操作系统的缺陷，覆盖移动和桌面环境。从多个分类维度分析缺陷分布（包括缺陷所在的OS层级、影响的功能、触发方式、严重程度以及修复涉及的代码元素），并进行维度间的联合分析和跨系统的相似性比较。

Result: 基于多维度分析和跨系统比较，总结出多项关于OS缺陷特征的重要发现，这些发现有助于全面理解不同操作系统中的缺陷规律。

Conclusion: 根植于研究发现，本文为更好的OS缺陷检测和调试提供了一系列可操作的启示，为未来旨在提升OS质量保障的研究提供了指导方针。

Abstract: Operating systems (OS) serve as the foundation for all other software systems, and thus defects in OSes can lead to severe conquences, such as system crashes and data corruption, affecting billions of users. This broad impact underscores the necessity and importance of ensuring OS quality. Understanding the characteristics of OS defects is a fundamental step in this quality assurance task, as it facilitates the design of effective defect detection and debugging approaches. In this work, we conduct a large-scale study of 1,500 defects from three distinct and representative operating systems (Android, Linux, and HarmonyOS) spanning both mobile and desktop environments. To the best of our knowledge, this is the largest study of its kind in this domain. By analyzing the distribution of OS defects across multiple classification dimensions, including the OS layer where defects occur, the functions they affect, how they are triggered, their severity, and the code elements involved in their repair, as well as performing joint analysis between dimensions and cross-OS similarity comparisons, we summarize several major findings that contribute to a comprehensive understanding of OS defects across systems. Based on these findings, we provide a series of actionable implications for better OS defect detection and debugging, offering guidelines for future research aimed at improving OS quality assurance.

</details>


### [246] [Temporal Validity on Real Software Histories: Eliminating Stale-Fact Errors in Code-Assistant Memory over GitHub Fixes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20685)
*Neeraj Yadav*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文验证了MemStrata——一种确定性(主体,关系,对象)取代记忆机制——在真实软件历史数据上解决RAG时间盲区问题。从707个真实GitHub issue中提取130个干净的原子状态转换,MemStrata达到0.91的答案准确率(对比RAG的0.57-0.59),并将RAG返回过时值的情况从36-38%降至约0%,同时保持RAG级别的检索延迟(约2.1秒)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG缺乏时间模型:当编码会话中事实发生变化(函数重命名、端点移动、依赖升级),RAG检索到新旧值且相似度几乎相同,无法区分当前值,导致返回过时信息。先前工作仅在合成单值基准上验证了确定性取代记忆机制,缺乏真实场景的端到端验证。

Method: 从SWE-bench Lite + Verified中的707个真实GitHub issue提取130个干净的原子状态转换(修复将一个可识别值从修复前形式变为修复后形式),渲染为无标记形式(过时与当前语句仅值不同)。在这些数据上对比MemStrata与RAG(含LLM重排序器变体)的性能。

Result: MemStrata达到0.91答案准确率,优于RAG的0.57-0.59;RAG被迫回答时36-38%的时间返回过时值(LLM重排序器无法改善),MemStrata将此降至约0;MemStrata延迟约2.1秒,显著优于重排序器的约18秒。研究期间发现并修复了一个真实产品缺陷(大小写/标点不敏感的值比较),同时保持了"moat属性"(干净代码突变上的确定性取代准确性)。

Conclusion: MemStrata在真实软件历史上验证了确定性取代记忆机制的有效性,在准确性和延迟上都显著优于RAG及LLM重排序器。但研究范围有限:仅约18%的真实修复属于干净原子转换,其余修复的提取覆盖是正交问题,留待后续工作。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) has no model of time: when a fact changes across a coding session - a function is renamed, an endpoint moves, a dependency is bumped - RAG retrieves both the old and new value with near-identical similarity and cannot tell which is current, so it serves the superseded value. Paper 1 showed, on synthetic single-value benchmarks, that a deterministic (subject, relation, object) supersession memory eliminates this failure. Here we validate it end-to-end on real software history. From 707 real GitHub issues (SWE-bench Lite + Verified) we extract 130 clean atomic state transitions, a fix that changes one identifiable value from a pre-fix to a post-fix form, and render each marker-free (the stale and current statements differ only in the value). On this set, MemStrata reaches 0.91 answer accuracy versus RAG's 0.57-0.59; and, the structural result, when forced to answer RAG serves the superseded value 36-38% of the time (an LLM reranker does not help) while MemStrata drives this to ~0, at RAG retrieval latency (~2.1 s vs ~18 s for the reranker). We are explicit about scope: only ~18% of real fixes are clean atomic transitions; Paper 2 isolates the memory mechanism on that class, and extraction coverage of the remaining fixes is the orthogonal problem we defer to follow-on work. A real product bug surfaced and was fixed during the study (a case/punctuation-insensitive value comparison), with the moat property (deterministic-supersession accuracy on clean code mutations) preserved and verified.

</details>


### [247] [An Extensive Empirical Study on Code Translation Technique](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20776)
*Ruihang Fan,Jiajun Jiang,Xinpeng Wang,Jiateng Fu,Fengjie Li,Jiasi Shen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对学习型与LLM型代码翻译技术进行了大规模实证研究，覆盖方法级和类级翻译、多种编程语言，评估了可执行正确性、代码相似度、翻译方向、翻译粒度和失败模式，发现LLM方法在方法级正确性上优于学习型方法，相似度指标不能可靠反映功能正确性，类级翻译难度显著更高，静态语义错误和逻辑错误是主要挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化代码翻译对软件演进日益重要，但学习型方法（learning-based）与LLM方法（LLM-based）之间的相对优势和局限性尚未得到充分理解，需要大规模实证研究来填补这一空白。

Method: 开展大规模实证研究，对比具有代表性的代码翻译技术（涵盖学习型方法、LLM型方法以及通用LLM），在多种编程语言的多语言方法级和类级基准上评估，分析维度包括可执行正确性、代码相似度、翻译方向、翻译粒度和失败模式。

Result: LLM和LLM型方法在方法级正确性上普遍优于学习型方法；相似度指标不能可靠反映功能正确性；翻译方向显著影响性能（尤其在不同类型系统特征的语言之间）；类级翻译比方法级翻译困难得多（需保留全局语义、接口、成员关系和跨方法依赖）；静态语义错误和逻辑错误是现有代码翻译系统的主要挑战。

Conclusion: 研究提供了实证证据和实践指导，为开发更健壮、类型感知、结构感知和上下文感知的代码翻译技术指明方向。

Abstract: Automated code translation is increasingly important for software evolution, yet the relative strengths and limitations of learning-based and large language model (LLM)-based techniques remain insufficiently understood. To address this gap, we conduct a large-scale empirical study comparing representative code translation techniques across methodological paradigms and translation granularities. We evaluate learning-based methods, LLM-based methods, and general-purpose LLMs on multilingual method-level and class-level benchmarks involving multiple programming languages. Our analysis considers executable correctness, code similarity, translation direction, translation granularity, and failure patterns. The results show that LLMs and LLM-based methods generally outperform learning-based methods in method-level correctness, although similarity metrics alone do not reliably reflect functional correctness. Translation direction substantially affects performance, particularly when translating between languages with different type-system characteristics. Class-level translation remains considerably more difficult than method-level translation because it requires preserving global semantics, interfaces, member relationships, and cross-method dependencies. Our error analysis further shows that static semantic errors and logical errors are the primary challenges in existing code translation systems. These findings provide empirical evidence and practical guidance for developing more robust, type-aware, structure-aware, and context-aware code translation techniques.

</details>


### [248] [BC-Bench: Evaluating Agentic Engineering in a Domain-Specific Language for ERP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20851)
*Haoran Sun,Klaus Marius Hansen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出BC-Bench，一个针对Microsoft Dynamics 365 Business Central的AL领域特定语言（DSL）的智能体工程基准测试，包含101个从生产仓库提取的手工任务，评估代码生成与测试生成能力，并发现通用基准上的改进并不能一致迁移到AL领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管智能体工程系统在通用基准上表现优异，但其在ERP领域特定语言（如AL）上的有效性尚未充分探索。现有评估缺乏针对该领域的真实任务基准，且AL生态面临公共资源有限和环境配置复杂等独特约束。

Method: 从两个微软生产仓库中提取101个手工策划任务，构建BC-Bench基准。改编SWE-Bench方法论以适应AL生态系统的独特约束，除生成功能代码外还评估测试生成能力，支持包含视觉上下文的多模态问题陈述，并在两个智能体框架上使用多轮运行指标评估多个前沿模型。

Result: 在Bug修复类别中，模型之间的解决率差异大于两个智能体框架之间的差异；通用基准上报告的改进并不能一致地迁移到AL领域。

Conclusion: 研究结果凸显了领域特定评估的必要性，表明通用基准的性能不能可靠预测ERP DSL任务中的表现。

Abstract: Agentic engineering systems have shown strong performance on general-purpose benchmarks, yet their effectiveness in enterprise resource planning (ERP) domain-specific languages (DSLs) remains underexplored. We introduce BC-Bench, a benchmark designed to evaluate agentic engineering on real-world tasks in AL, the DSL for Microsoft Dynamics 365 Business Central. BC-Bench comprises 101 manually curated tasks extracted from two Microsoft-owned production repositories, reflecting authentic ERP development workflows. Adapting the SWE-Bench methodology, we address the unique constraints of the AL ecosystem---including limited public resources and complex environment provisioning. Beyond generating functional code, BC-Bench evaluates test generation and supports multimodal problem statements where visual context is commonly present. We evaluate multiple frontier models across two agent harnesses, utilizing multi-run metrics to account for nondeterminism. In the Bug Fixing category, under our evaluated settings, between-model differences in resolution rate are larger than differences between the two evaluated agent harnesses, and improvements reported on general-purpose benchmarks do not consistently transfer to AL. These results highlight the need for domain-specific evaluation.

</details>


### [249] [Beyond the Traceback: Using LLMs for Adaptive Explanations of Programming Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20896)
*Alexandru-Radu Moraru,Shreyan Biswas,Ujwal Gadiraju*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 这项研究通过多阶段众包实验（N=103）评估了针对不同技能水平定制的、由LLM生成的Python错误信息。研究发现，虽然LLM重写的错误信息显著改善了主观评价（实用型信息被认为更清晰、认知负担更低），但这些感知上的改善并未转化为客观调试性能的显著提升，揭示了一个关键的人机互补性鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编程错误信息对软件开发至关重要，但新手程序员难以理解。虽然LLM可以将错误信息改写得更清晰，但尚不清楚提高可读性是否真的能改善客观调试性能，以及解释风格应如何与程序员技能水平相匹配。

Method: 采用多阶段众包研究设计，通过自定义能力评估将103名参与者按技能水平分类，针对标准解释器信息与两种LLM生成的错误信息风格（实用型/行动导向型和条件型/支架式解释）进行对比测试，同时测量客观调试指标（修复率、尝试次数、修复时间）和主观感知指标（可读性、认知负荷、语气）。

Result: LLM重写的错误信息显著改善了主观评价，实用型信息被评为更清晰、认知负担更低；但这些感知上的改善并未转化为客观调试性能的统计学显著提升。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了一个关键的人机互补性鸿沟：感觉更好的解释并不一定能让用户成为更有效的调试者。未来工具应从业态化的静态技能定制改写转向基于用户实时修复轨迹的动态调整。

Abstract: Programming error messages are critical for software development, yet they remain difficult for novice programmers to interpret. While Large Language Models (LLMs) can rewrite these errors into clearer explanations, it remains unclear whether increased readability improves objective debugging performance or how explanation styles should align with programmer skill. We present a multi-stage crowdsourced study N=103 evaluating skill-targeted, LLM-generated Python error messages. Using a custom proficiency assessment, we categorized participants by skill level and tested standard interpreter messages against two LLM-generated styles: pragmatic (action-oriented) and contingent (scaffolded explanations). We measured both objective debugging metrics (fix rate, attempts, time-to-fix) and subjective perceptions (readability, cognitive load, tone). Our results show that while LLM-rewritten messages significantly improved subjective evaluations, with pragmatic messages rated as clearer and less cognitively demanding, these perceived gains did not translate into statistically significant improvements in objective debugging performance. This highlights a critical human-AI complementarity gap: explanations that feel better to users do not necessarily make them more effective debuggers. We discuss design implications for adaptive AI feedback systems, arguing that future tools should pivot from static skill-targeted rewriting toward dynamic adjustments based on a user's real-time repair trajectory.

</details>


### [250] [Generation of Web Apps with Agentic IDEs: An Empirical Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20903)
*Manuel Marceca,Maria Teresa Rossi,Leonardo Mariani*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对三种主流 Agentic IDE（Copilot、Cursor、Windsurf）在从零生成五个全栈 Web 应用的任务上进行了严格的比较分析。结果表明，这些工具在生成成熟模式（如 CRUD 操作和认证功能）方面表现出较高成熟度，但在生成较少见的分布式架构（如任务队列架构）时错误显著增多。总体来看，Agentic IDE 无法完全替代开发者，而是将开发者的角色转变为通过自然语言指令和迭代优化来编排 LLM 智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic IDE 是软件工程领域最重要的创新之一，旨在通过基于 LLM 的智能体加速应用开发。然而，它们在端到端开发任务（即生成完整应用）中的评估仍然有限，因此需要系统的比较研究来填补这一空白。

Method: 作者对三种流行的 Agentic IDE（Copilot、Cursor 和 Windsurf）进行了严格的比较分析，评估它们在从零生成五个全栈 Web 应用方面的表现。评估覆盖了不同的应用类型，包括成熟模式（CRUD、认证）和较不常见的分布式架构。

Result: 1) Agentic IDE 在生成成熟模式（CRUD、认证）方面表现成熟；2) 在生成不常见的分布式架构（如任务队列）时错误显著增多；3) 各 Agentic IDE 各有特点，但整体差异不大；4) Agentic IDE 不能完全替代开发者。

Conclusion: Agentic IDE 不能完全取代开发者，但改变了开发者的角色——从手写代码转向通过自然语言和迭代优化来编排 LLM 智能体构建软件。这些工具在成熟模式上表现可靠，但在复杂或不常见的架构生成上仍需改进。

Abstract: Agentic IDEs are among the most significant innovations in software engineering, aiming to accelerate application development through LLM-based agents that can assist developers during development. However, their evaluation in end-to-end development tasks involving the generation of complete applications remains limited. To fill this gap, we propose a rigorous comparative analysis of three popular agentic IDEs (Copilot, Cursor, and Windsurf) in the generation of five full-stack Web applications from scratch.
  Results show high maturity in the generation of established patterns, such as CRUD operations and authentication features. In contrast, the generation of less common distributed architectures, such as a task queue architecture, produces significantly more errors. Overall, results show that Agentic IDEs cannot replace developers but shift their role toward building software by orchestrating LLM-based agents through natural-language instructions and iterative refinement. Yet, each agentic IDE shows its peculiarities, although differences are narrow.

</details>


### [251] [Spike-Killer: Evidence-Gated LLM Assistance for Safe Performance Diagnosis on a Real Windows Workstation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21069)
*Baocheng Zeng,Jinhao Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍Spike-Killer，一个以人为批准的诊断工作流，用于处理真实Windows工作站上的帧时间（frame-time）投诉。该工作流将每个LLM助手动作视为"证据门控事务"，包含状态记录、风险分类、快照保存、后置条件验证等步骤。作者在Counter-Strike 2上完成了同日研究，产生了90.69秒和85.85秒的GPU跟踪记录，但明确声明研究贡献在于可审计、人在环路中的模式，而非性能数据本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM辅助代理虽能综合系统证据、提议配置更改并自动化诊断任务，但其灵活性导致不精确的动作或侵入式收集器可能成为运营风险。需要一个受控、可审计、以人为中心的工作流来降低这些风险。

Method: 将每个代理动作设计为证据门控事务：记录精确目标状态→分类风险→保存快照→验证后置条件→将失败测量作为一等证据保留。在真实Windows工作站上以CS2为目标应用进行经验性研究，使用Windows Performance Recorder进行系统级追踪。

Result: 证据包包含状态快照、微基准测试、十次同状态重复性探针、实时遥测、修复的过宽注册表操作、不兼容的演示捕获尝试、无效的本地回放以及系统级追踪替代方案。WPR产生两条CS2本地Bot GPU跟踪（90.69秒和85.85秒），归因于cs2.exe，暴露了DxgKrnl Present元数据，零丢失ETW缓冲或事件。但研究未报告帧间隔、P99估计或干预效果。

Conclusion: 该工作的贡献是一种可审计、人在环路的模式，用于在真实工作站上实现可信赖的代理辅助，包括在证据不足时明确设置停止条件。研究强调追踪完整性而非性能诊断结果本身。

Abstract: LLM-assisted agents can synthesize system evidence, propose configuration changes, and automate diagnostic tasks, but their flexibility makes an imprecise action or an intrusive collector an operational risk. We present Spike-Killer, a human-approved workflow for diagnosing frame-time complaints on one real Windows workstation. The workflow treats each action as an evidence-gated transaction: it records the exact target state, classifies risk, preserves a snapshot, verifies a postcondition, and retains failed measurements as first-class evidence.
  This experience paper reports a completed same-day study with Counter-Strike 2 as a demanding target application. The evidence bundle contains preserved state snapshots, exploratory microbenchmarks, a ten-run same-state repeatability probe, live telemetry, a repaired over-broad registry action, incompatible presentation-capture attempts, an invalid local replay, and a system-level tracing replacement. Windows Performance Recorder produced two CS2 local-Bot GPU traces of 90.69 and 85.85 seconds; both were attributed to cs2.exe, exposed DxgKrnl Present metadata, and had zero lost ETW buffers or events. These results qualify trace integrity, not performance: the study reports no frame intervals, P99 estimate, or intervention effect. The contribution is an auditable, human-in-the-loop pattern for trustworthy agent assistance on a real workstation, including explicit stop conditions when evidence is insufficient.

</details>


### [252] [Trustworthy RAG: An Evaluation Agent for Detecting Misinformation and Knowledge Poisoning in Generative AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21095)
*Balkrishna Giri,Md Toufique Hasan,Jussi Rasku,Muhammad Waseem,Pekka Abrahamsson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个评估代理（Evaluation Agent），作为RAG系统的中间件，结合NLI事实验证、五信号投毒检测器和加权信任指数（T = 0.4F + 0.35C + 0.25(1-P)），以弥合RAG系统的"安全-可靠性鸿沟"（语义相关不等于事实真实）。在TruthfulQA上达到91%准确率和100%精确率，指令注入检测召回率达100%；信任指数在三个LLM上保持判别力（ROC-AUC 0.73-0.81），但在跨数据集泛化（FEVER）和困难攻击（实体替换、语义弱化）上表现有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统通常盲目信任检索结果，存在安全-可靠性鸿沟：高语义相关性不保证事实真实性。攻击者可通过知识投毒插入恶意文档，诱导LLM产生定向错误信息，现有方法缺乏系统的投毒检测机制。

Method: 提出一个评估代理（中间件），包含三个核心组件：(1) NLI事实验证模块；(2) 五信号投毒检测器（结合相关性加权聚合）；(3) 信任指数T = 0.4F + 0.35C + 0.25(1-P)，对高污染上下文使用非线性阻尼器。代理在生成前测量上下文的投毒检测情况。

Result: 在TruthfulQA + Llama 3.3 70B上：91%准确率、100%精确率，指令注入召回率100%；信任指数跨三个LLM保持判别力（ROC-AUC 0.73-0.81）；安全编码助手场景下阻碍不安全指令注入（F1 92%）；但实体替换等原位编辑和语义弱化仍难以检测，FEVER跨数据集泛化需领域特定校准。

Conclusion: 评估代理能有效检测RAG上下文中的投毒攻击（尤其是指令注入和高置信投毒），但存在局限：原位编辑攻击难以检测、跨数据集泛化需针对性校准、生成风格比模型大小对检测影响更大。代理测量的是生成前的上下文投毒检测，而非LLM是否实际采用了注入的错误信息。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) grounds Large Language Model (LLM) outputs in external knowledge, but RAG systems usually trust whatever they retrieve, creating a Security-Reliability Gap: high semantic relevance does not guarantee factual truth. Adversaries exploit this through knowledge poisoning, inserting malicious documents to cause targeted misinformation. We propose an Evaluation Agent, middleware that combines Natural Language Inference (NLI) factual verification, a five-signal poison detector with relevance-weighted aggregation, and a Trust Index T = 0.4 F + 0.35 C + 0.25 (1 - P ) with a non-linear dampener for high-contamination contexts. On TruthfulQA with Llama 3.3 70B, the agent reaches 91% accuracy and 100% precision, with 100% recall on instruction injection, while in-place edits, such as entity swaps, remain hard to detect. Across three LLMs the Trust Index stays discriminative, with a Receiver Operating Characteristic Area Under the Curve (ROC-AUC) of 0.73 to 0.81; generation style matters more than model size, and per-LLM threshold calibration restores baseline competitive accuracy, whereas a weaker FEVER result shows that cross-dataset generalization requires domain-specific calibration. In a software-engineering use case, a secure-coding assistant over guidance from the Open Worldwide Application Security Project (OWASP) Top 10 and the Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE), the agent reliably blocks instruction injection of unsafe advice (F1 92%), while contradiction and subtle semantic weakening remain hard. Throughout, the agent measures detection of poisoned context before generation, not whether the LLM adopts the injected misinformation. We release the proposed approach, attack generator, and experimental artifacts at the link: https://github.com/GPT-Laboratory/TrustworthyRAG.

</details>


### [253] [On the Time and Frequency Domain Representations of Signals for CPS Specification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21167)
*Claudio Mandrioli,Drishti Yadav,Domenico Bianculli*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出信号-频谱时序逻辑（S2TL），一种利用时频表示来指定CPS需求的规范语言。它通过分析现有CPS需求分类法，识别适合时频表示的类别，定义了S2TL的语义、监控器和规范模板。实验表明，相比时域规范，时频规范可扩展到线性插值轨迹并提高对偏移和高频噪声的容忍度，同时保持相当的保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CPS规范语言大多使用信号的时域表示，难以描述信号形状和动态行为。而控制和机器人领域常使用频域表示来刻画这些行为。时频表示结合了两者的优势，但目前缺乏使用时频表示来指定CPS需求的规范语言。

Method: 1) 分析现有CPS需求分类法，识别可从时频表示获益的需求类别；2) 推导使用时频表示的规范语言设计准则；3) 提出S2TL语言，支持对频率区间和频率分量间关系的断言；4) 操作化S2TL语义用于监控CPS轨迹并实现监控器；5) 为识别的需求类别定义规范模板；6) 对比时域与时频域公式的适用性、表达保真度和噪声容忍度。

Result: 时域规范仅适用于包含阶跃变化或使用常数插值的输入轨迹，而时频规范将评估扩展到线性插值生成的轨迹，并改善了对偏移和高频噪声的容忍度，同时实现了与预期系统属性相当的保真度。

Conclusion: 时频表示能够扩展CPS规范语言的适用性并提高噪声容忍度，S2TL作为一种利用时频表示的规范语言，在监控CPS需求方面展现出优于传统时域方法的能力。

Abstract: Specification languages are instrumental to the Verification \& Validation of Cyber-Physical Systems (CPSs). Most state-of-the-art specification languages use the time-domain representation of signals, which is not always suitable for describing signal shapes and dynamic behaviours. Instead, fields like control and robotics use the frequency-domain representation to characterise these behaviours. Time-frequency representations combine the capabilities of both domains. We investigate the use of time-frequency representations to specify CPS requirements. We analyse existing taxonomies of CPS requirements to identify which requirement classes can benefit from time-frequency representations. We derive the desiderata for a specification language that uses time-frequency representations and propose Signal-Spectrum Temporal Logic (S2TL), a language enabling assertions over frequency intervals and relations between frequency components. We operationalise the S2TL semantics for monitoring CPS traces, and implement a monitor. We define specification templates for the identified requirement classes and compare time- and time-frequency-domain formulations in terms of applicability, expression fidelity, and noise tolerance of monitoring. We observe that, while time-domain specifications are applicable only to input traces containing step-like changes or using constant interpolation, time-frequency specifications extend their evaluation to traces generated through linear interpolation, and improve tolerance to offset and high-frequency noise, while achieving comparable fidelity to the intended system properties.

</details>


### [254] [Specification Portability Across LLM Development Agents: Cross-Agent Compatibility in Specification-Driven Software Migration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21208)
*Oleg Grynets,Oleksii Ilchuk,Dariia Zatulna,Vasyl Lyashkevych*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过Oracle到PostgreSQL迁移这一受控软件转换任务，研究跨智能体规范可移植性问题。实验分为两个阶段：首先对1,006个PL/SQL文件进行规范优先迁移管线评估，其次在1,802个Oracle脚本数据集上使用多个AI智能体（Amazon Kiro、Google Gemini、GitHub Copilot、Claude Code、Cursor）进行跨智能体实验。结果表明规范大小不能预测实现质量，跨智能体转移可能导致严重退化（最严重案例Token F1仅0.035），规范不应被视为智能体中性的产物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多智能体软件开发（SDD）工作流中，一个智能体生成的规范可能被另一个智能体用于实现，但规范是否具有跨智能体可移植性（即被其他智能体正确理解和使用）尚未被充分研究。该论文旨在填补这一空白，揭示规范在不同智能体间的转移是否会引入质量退化。

Method: 采用两阶段实验设计：(1) 规范优先迁移管线在1,006个PL/SQL文件上评估（623个成功再生成，380个生成脚本在PostgreSQL 16中成功执行）；(2) 跨智能体实验使用1,802个Oracle脚本及对应PostgreSQL实现，涉及Amazon Kiro、Google Gemini、GitHub Copilot（Claude Code和Cursor参与初始单智能体评估），评估指标包括Token F1、精确匹配、SQL语法有效性、AST精确匹配、AST平均相似度和即时可运行性。还测试了重写、压缩和检索增强摄入（RAG）等策略。

Result: 规范大小不是实现质量的可靠预测因子；跨智能体转移产生显著的智能体依赖退化。最严重的复现案例是Gemini直接消耗Kiro来源规范，Token F1仅0.035、SQL语法有效性2.33%、AST平均相似度0.015。重写能显著改善Gemini在测试配置下的表现，压缩无普遍收益，检索增强摄入是唯一同时出现在Gemini和Copilot的Pareto前沿上的通用策略。

Conclusion: 异构SDD工作流中的规范不应被自动视为智能体中立的工件。研究呼吁在多智能体软件工程中明确考虑规范可移植性、智能体特定解释以及基于检索的访问机制。

Abstract: This paper investigates cross-agent specification portability using Oracle-to-PostgreSQL migration as a controlled software transformation task. The study combines two experimental stages. First, a specification-first migration pipeline was evaluated on 1,006 PL/SQL files, of which 623 were successfully regenerated and 380 generated scripts executed successfully in PostgreSQL 16. Second, cross-agent experiments were conducted on a dataset of 1,802 Oracle scripts with corresponding PostgreSQL implementations using Amazon Kiro, Google Gemini, and GitHub Copilot, with Claude Code and Cursor included in the initial single-agent evaluation. Native and foreign specifications were assessed using Token F1, exact match, SQL syntax validity, AST exact match, AST mean similarity, and immediate runnability. The results show that specification size alone does not predict implementation quality and that cross-agent transfer can produce substantial agent-dependent degradation. The strongest replicated case occurred when Gemini directly consumed a Kiro-origin specification, producing a Token F1 of 0.035, SQL syntax validity of 2.33%, and AST mean similarity of 0.015. Rewriting substantially improved Gemini in the tested configuration, compression did not provide a universal benefit, and retrieval-augmented ingestion was the only common strategy represented on the per-agent Pareto frontiers of both Gemini and Copilot. The findings suggest that specifications in heterogeneous SDD workflows should not automatically be treated as agent-neutral artifacts and motivate explicit consideration of specification portability, agent-specific interpretation, and retrieval-based access in multi-agent software engineering.

</details>


### [255] [The Substitution Escrow Threshold: When "Compatible With" Becomes Safe Enough to Buy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21221)
*Amadeus Brandes*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"替换托管阈值"（Substitution Escrow Threshold）框架，包含五个条件，用于评估兼容性声明是否真正降低机构风险还是仅降低首次集成成本，并应用于五个基础设施案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业基础设施买家常将兼容性声明（如"S3兼容"、"PostgreSQL兼容"）作为未来替换选项的代理指标，但大多数兼容性声明并未真正托管其所暗示的替换路径，需要一种系统化框架来区分真正的风险降低与单纯的初次集成成本降低。

Method: 提出五条件框架：边界闭合（boundary closure）、可执行一致性（executable conformance）、托管独立性（custody independence）、状态与操作可逆性（state and operations reversibility）、扩展隔离（extension quarantine）。将五个基础设施案例（OCI、Kubernetes、OpenTelemetry、S3、PostgreSQL）映射到五个不同的结果单元中进行分析。

Result: 该框架为企业架构师、平台工程师、采购团队和投资者提供了可操作的诊断工具，用于评估依赖兼容性的基础设施决策。

Conclusion: 该框架识别出AI基础设施是下一个最紧迫的应用领域，兼容性声明在AI基础设施中将面临更大的挑战和更迫切的分析需求。

Abstract: Enterprise infrastructure buyers routinely evaluate compatibility claims--"S3-compatible," "PostgreSQL-compatible," "OpenAI compatible"--as proxies for future substitution options. Yet most compatibility claims do not escrow the substitution path they imply. This paper introduces the Substitution Escrow Threshold, a five-condition framework that determines when a compatibility claim genuinely reduces institutional risk versus merely reducing first-integration cost. The five conditions--boundary closure, executable conformance, custody independence, state and operations reversibility, and extension quarantine--are applied to five infrastructure cases (OCI, Kubernetes, OpenTelemetry, S3, PostgreSQL) that populate five distinct outcome cells. The framework produces actionable diagnostics for enterprise architects, platform engineers, procurement teams, and investors evaluating compatibility-dependent infrastructure decisions, and identifies AI infrastructure as the framework's most urgent next application.

</details>


### [256] [Human-AI Collaboration in Requirements Engineering: Evidence of the Negative Effect of LLMs on Requirements Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21298)
*Giovanna Broccia,Julian Frattini,Chetan Arora,Maurice H. ter Beek,Alessandro Fantechi,Andreas Vogelsang,Alessio Ferrari*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过34名参与者的交叉设计对照实验，研究了LLM支持对人类需求检查(RI)的影响。结果显示LLM支持会负面影响气味检测准确性，但对分类和耗时无显著影响，且先使用LLM支持会减缓技能习得。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM在需求工程领域潜力巨大，但关于LLM作为人类需求检查协作助手的实证证据仍然稀缺，缺乏对其真实影响的研究。

Method: 采用受控交叉设计实验，34名参与者分别在有无LLM支持下检查文本规格，识别和分类需求气味并记录时间。使用贝叶斯回归模型分析各结果变量，控制交叉设计带来的效度威胁、协变量和中介变量。

Result: LLM支持对气味检测准确性有负面影响，但对气味分类和任务时长无显著影响。实验期间存在学习效应，但当检查首次在LLM支持下进行时，学习效应减弱。

Conclusion: 实证证据表明LLM支持不一定能提升性能，反而可能阻碍新手检查者的表现；从开始就使用LLM支持学习需求检查可能减慢技能获取，对LLM支持的学习方式构成威胁。

Abstract: Background. Requirements inspection (RI) is a well-established practice for detecting potential defects in requirements artifacts early in the software lifecycle. Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have stimulated interest in their potential to support requirements engineering (RE) tasks. However, empirical evidence on the effects of LLMs when used as collaborative assistants in human-performed RI remains scarce. Aims. We aim to investigate the impact of LLM support on human-performed RI, considering inspection effectiveness in terms of smell identification and severity classification (i.e., nocuous vs innocuous), as well as inspection duration. Method. We conducted a controlled crossover design experiment with 34 participants, who inspected textual specifications with and without LLM support, identifying and classifying requirements smells while recording inspection time. We analyzed the data using one Bayesian regression model per outcome variable, accounting for validity threats induced by the crossover design as well as covariates and mediators. Results. Results show that LLM support negatively affects smell detection accuracy but has no significant effect on smell classification or task duration. A learning effect is present across experimental periods, but reduced when RI is first performed with LLM support. Conclusions. Our findings provide empirical evidence that LLM support does not necessarily improve performance and may, instead, hinder it for novice inspectors. Moreover, the results suggest that learning RI with LLM-support from the beginning may slow down the skill acquisition process, implying threats for LLM-supported learning.

</details>


### [257] [Beyond Fault Localization: A Trajectory-Level Study of LLM Agents for Microservice Root Cause Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21310)
*Qisheng Lu,Aoyang Fang,Junjielong Xu,Jin'ao Shang,Songhan Zhang,Yifan Yang,Xiaochuan Yan,Pinjia He*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对微服务自动根因分析（RCA）提出轨迹级评估框架，将RCA视为可观测的诊断过程，而非仅关注端点正确性。通过分析3,500条诊断轨迹，揭示了答案正确性与诊断质量之间的脱节，并据此提出DiagGuard防御性架构，将Acc@1从43.5%提升至52.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RCA评估仅以端点正确性（是否定位责任服务）为指标，无法反映诊断的证据基础及故障传播路径，而这两者正是值班SRE判断是否采取行动所需的关键信息。因此需要一个能评估诊断过程本身质量的框架。

Method: 1) 提出轨迹级评估框架，将Agent执行过程与人工标注的服务级故障传播路径进行对比分析；2) 在公开微服务RCA基准上分析3,500条诊断轨迹，刻画Agent的探查位置与遥测数据使用方式；3) 归纳出成功与失败诊断的行为特征分类；4) 基于该分类法设计DiagGuard两阶段纵深防御架构：grounding阶段先调查可用观测，localization后verification阶段依据观测审计诊断。

Result: 发现答案正确性与诊断质量之间存在脱节：Agent可能定位到故障源但无法重构其传播路径。成功诊断的特征是停留在故障影响面、依据检索证据行动、随搜索深入扩展查询范围。失败源于关键证据遗漏、证据误读或以不支持推断替代缺失证据。在独立设置（不同模型、基准、拓扑）下DiagGuard将Acc@1从43.5%提升至52.5%。

Conclusion: 轨迹级评估能揭示最终答案指标所隐藏的局限，并为改进自动化RCA提供可操作指导。诊断过程的质量与最终答案的正确性同等重要，需要同时评估和优化。

Abstract: Existing evaluations of automated root cause analysis (RCA) for microservices assess diagnostic performance mainly by endpoint correctness: whether a method localizes the responsible service. This criterion enables comparison but does not reveal the evidentiary basis of a diagnosis or the fault-propagation route connecting the source to observed symptoms, both of which an on-call site reliability engineer needs to judge whether action is warranted. We therefore treat RCA as an observable diagnostic process. Our trajectory-level framework evaluates agent executions against manually curated service-level fault-propagation paths. Applied to a public microservice RCA benchmark, it analyzes 3,500 diagnostic trajectories, characterizing where agents investigate and how they use retrieved telemetry. We find a disconnect between answer correctness and diagnostic quality: an agent may localize the fault source yet fail to reconstruct its propagation. Successful investigations stay on the fault-impact surface, act on retrieved evidence, and broaden their query repertoire as the search deepens. Failures arise when decisive evidence is omitted, retrieved evidence is misinterpreted, or unsupported inference substitutes for missing evidence. We operationalize this taxonomy as DiagGuard, a two-stage defense-in-depth architecture in which grounding surveys available observations before localization and verification audits the diagnosis against them. In an independent setting with a different model, benchmark, and service topology, DiagGuard raises Acc@1 from 43.5% to 52.5%. These results show that trajectory-level evaluation exposes limitations hidden by final-answer metrics and provides actionable guidance for improving automated RCA.

</details>


### [258] [AI-to-AI Code Reviews of GitHub Pull Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21311)
*Niruthiha Selvanayagam,Taher A. Ghaleb*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个大规模AI-to-AI代码评审数据集，通过链接CodAGE中的AI归属PR与AI归属评审事件，分析了248,641个独特的AI归属PR及其AI评审。研究发现跨产品AI-to-AI评审在2025年Q1至Q3增长了两个数量级以上，但仅占识别到的agent编写PR的约1.6%。评审输出因作者-评审者配置而异，同产品配置下评论数普遍较高，跨产品评审中位延迟为1.2分钟，同产品为4.7分钟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI编码代理在软件开发工作流中的广泛应用，AI既作为PR作者又作为PR评审者，形成了AI评审AI的闭环。但目前缺乏大规模的数据来分析这一现象，因此作者希望构建数据集并系统性地刻画AI-to-AI评审的规模、趋势和特征。

Method: 利用CodAGE这一公开的编码代理生成GitHub事件数据集，将AI归属的PR与AI归属的评审事件进行链接，构建大规模的AI-to-AI代码评审数据集。数据集包含248,641个独特的AI归属PR，并将其分为跨产品评审（45,269个）和同产品评审（208,145个），另有4,773个PR同时接受两种评审。作者对比分析了不同作者-评审者配置下的评审输出、评论数量及延迟等指标。

Result: 数据集包含248,641个独特的AI归属PR。跨产品AI-to-AI评审占识别到的agent编写PR的约1.6%，但绝对数量可观，且从2025年Q1到Q3增长了超过两个数量级。CodeRabbit对Claude Code编写的PR标注35.0%的评论为重构评论，而对Copilot编写的PR仅为10.5%（但可能反映PR特征而非评审者差异）。四位双重角色评审者中有三位在同产品组中平均每PR评论数高出58-65%，但效应量小且集中于上尾分布。跨产品对的中位延迟为1.2分钟，同产品对为4.7分钟，但时间戳可用性和评审者组成差异限制了比较的有效性。

Conclusion: 闭环AI-to-AI评审正在增长但仍属于识别到的agent活动中的少数。评审输出因作者agent组和产品配置的不同而变化。研究提供了首个大规模刻画AI评审AI现象的实证数据集和分析，为理解AI代理在软件开发中的闭环互动提供了基础。

Abstract: AI coding agents are increasingly integrated into software development workflows, operating on both sides of the pull-request (PR) process: AI authoring agents create or modify PRs, while AI reviewers evaluate them. This creates a closed loop in which one AI coding agent reviews a contribution attributed to another. We construct a large-scale dataset of AI-to-AI code review by linking AI-attributed PRs with AI-attributed review events from CodAGE, a public dataset of coding-agent-generated GitHub events. Our dataset contains 248,641 unique AI-attributed PRs that received at least one AI-attributed review. Of these, 45,269 received cross-product review and 208,145 received same-product review; 4,773 PRs received both. Cross-product AI-to-AI review occurred in approximately 1.6% of identified agent-authored PRs but was substantial in absolute terms, and its volume increased by more than two orders of magnitude from 2025-Q1 to 2025-Q3. Reviewer output varied across author-reviewer configurations. CodeRabbit labeled 35.0% of its comments on Claude Code-authored PRs as refactor comments, compared with 10.5% on Copilot-authored PRs, although this difference may reflect characteristics of the PRs rather than the reviewer. For three of four dual-role reviewers, mean comments per PR were 58-65% higher in the same-product group, although effect sizes were small or negligible and the difference was concentrated in the upper tail. Among pairs with complete, nonnegative timestamps, the observed median latency was 1.2 minutes for cross-product pairs and 4.7 minutes for same-product pairs; differential timestamp availability and reviewer composition limit this comparison. Overall, closed-loop AI-to-AI review is increasing but remains a minority of identified agent activity, with review output varying across authoring-agent groups and product configurations.

</details>


### [259] [Natural-Language Workflows Are Not Software Yet: Artifact-Driven Compilation for Reliable Agent Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21341)
*Xiangzhe Xu,Hanxi Guo,Guangyu Shen,Siyuan Cheng,Xiangyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Artic，一个工件驱动的工作流编译器，将自然语言工作流转换为显式声明数据依赖、约束和控制流的工件驱动形式，通过约束优化精炼难以执行的步骤，并利用局部义务分解和场景化试运行验证编译忠实性，在11个真实领域工作流的488个实例上将任务解决率提升28个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言工作流为智能体提供了类似软件的接口，但该承诺尚不可靠：工作流描述常隐含数据依赖，执行者需推断步骤应使用哪些先前结果；智能体在上下文压力下也容易无法遵循长或分支指令。

Method: Artic将自然语言工作流编译为工件驱动形式，其中每步显式声明读写的工件，约束门控产生的工件，显式控制转移路由执行。该表示暴露执行负担，使编译器能识别过度依赖状态或含困难控制逻辑的步骤并通过约束优化精炼。验证方面，Artic将忠实性检查分解为局部义务，并使用基于场景的试运行测试编译区域是否符合源工作流。

Result: 在11个真实领域工作流的488个问题实例上，相比原始文本工作流，任务解决率提升28个百分点；编译后的工作流在跨模型和重复执行设置下的一致性分别提升32和56个百分点。

Conclusion: Artic通过显式化数据依赖和控制流，并系统性地精炼难以执行的步骤，显著提升了自然语言工作流在智能体执行中的可靠性和一致性。

Abstract: Natural-language workflows offer a software-like interface for agents: domain experts can write reusable procedures, and agents can execute them as instructions. This promise is not yet reliable. Workflow descriptions often leave data dependencies implicit, so the executor must infer which prior results a step should use; agents can also fail to follow long or branching instructions under context pressure. We propose Artic, an artifact-driven workflow compiler that transforms a natural-language workflow into an artifact-driven workflow in which each step declares the artifacts it reads and writes, constraints gate produced artifacts, and explicit control transfers route execution. This representation exposes the enforcement burden placed on agent execution, allowing the compiler to identify steps that depend on too much state or contain difficult control logic and refine them through constrained optimization. To validate the LLM-assisted transformation, Artic decomposes faithfulness checking into local obligations and uses scenario-based dry runs to test whether compiled workflow regions conform to the source workflow. We evaluate Artic on 488 problem instances from 11 real-world domain workflows; it improves task resolve rate by 28 percentage points over the original text workflow. We also show that workflows compiled by Artic are 32 and 56 percentage points more consistent in cross-model and repeated-execution setups, respectively.

</details>


### [260] [AI with Authority, from Application to Silicon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21356)
*Jason Hickey*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文报告了一个颠覆性发现：生成式AI颠覆了传统"机器验证昂贵"的认知——在AI速度下，机器验证不仅经济实惠，更是提高生产力的关键。一位研究员在五周内仅用消费级AI订阅，指挥一支AI智能体舰队，从应用代码经验证编译器一路到流片的RISC-V处理器，全程零人工审查证明、零人工编写RTL。其工作纪律(Salt方法)依赖证明内核，任何幻觉证明都无法通过。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 六十年以来，机器验证一直是高昂的成本负担，仅适用于极少数特殊工件。本文旨在证明生成式AI时代这一关系被彻底逆转：机器验证成为AI高速工作中不可或缺的"可信裁判"，使得单人能够安全地规模化指挥自主机器工作。

Method: 采用Salt方法作为工作纪律：以证明内核为根基，数学声明以内核检查过的工件形式在智能体间传递，人类注意力仅保留在陈述、设计与裁决上。验证按环节逐一声明，从Lean 4内核到硅边界处的SAT检查等价。全程记录定理来源、预注册token计量、人类时间下限，以及编号至#256的错误账本（一个单调计数器，基于追加式标志账本）。

Result: 在五周内，一位研究员通过消费级AI订阅，指挥一小队AI智能体，完成了从应用代码、经验证编译器与执行环境，到在社区硅穿梭机上流片RISC-V处理器的完整流程。全程无证明经人工审查，无RTL由人类编写。错误账本计数达#256（其中#79从未分配，后续捕获未编号记录），而到达记录的证明中，不正确的证明数量为零。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了：在生成式AI时代，机器验证从奢侈品变为必需品，是让独立个体安全指挥大规模自主AI工作的关键基础设施。Salt方法的成功表明"证明内核+人类聚焦高层决策"的模式能够实现零差错的大规模AI驱动硬件开发。

Abstract: For sixty years, machine verification has been a major cost overhead, affordable only for exceptional artifacts. Here we report that generative AI inverts this relationship: at AI speed, machine verification is not only economical but essential to productivity --- it is the incorruptible referee that lets one person safely direct autonomous machine work at scale. In five weeks, one researcher on consumer AI subscriptions directed a small fleet of AI agents from application code, through a verified compiler and executive, to a RISC-V processor taped out on a community silicon shuttle; no proof passed through human review, and no RTL was written by a human. The working discipline --- the Salt method --- rests on a proof kernel no hallucinated proof can pass: mathematical claims travel between agents as kernel-checked artifacts, and human attention is reserved for statements, designs, and rulings. Verification is stated link by link, from the Lean 4 kernel to SAT-checked equivalence at the silicon boundary. We publish the complete accounting: theorem provenance, a pre-registered token meter, floor-bounded human time, and an error ledger whose catch numbering runs to #256 --- a monotone counter over the mathematics campaign's append-only flags ledger, maintained 2026-07-07 to 2026-07-20 (one number, #79, was never assigned; later catches are recorded un-numbered) --- against zero incorrect proofs reaching the record.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [261] [Towards Traffic Modelling of Multi-Agent Systems: The Role of Coordination Topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20494)
*Davide Lamagna,Albert Cabellos,Alberto Rodriguez-Natal,Gábor Rétvári,Berta Serracanta*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文对多智能体LLM系统的流量模式进行了实证研究，通过多层测量框架在三种协调拓扑（顺序、星型、全网状）上各执行500次重复运行，发现拓扑结构从根本上塑造了到达LLM后端的请求过程：扇出协调引入了顺序执行中不存在的结构双峰性，推理阶段的组件最佳用对数正态分布描述，泊松指数零模型在所有拓扑中被明确拒绝。这些差异进一步传播到推理和网络层指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统作为新兴的网络化工作负载，其内部生成的请求流量模式与传统的由用户驱动的应用不同——单个用户任务可引发结构化的模型调用序列，其时序由协调逻辑而非用户到达率决定。经典流量模型是否适用于此场景尚不明确，需要实证研究来理解其流量特征。

Method: 采用多层测量框架，对顺序（sequential）、星型（star）和全网状（full-mesh）三种智能体协调拓扑各进行500次重复运行，测量并刻画LLM调用间到达时间（interarrival time）分布，然后在推理和网络层分析这些差异的传播效应。

Result: 发现拓扑从根本上塑造到达过程：扇出协调引入结构性的双峰分布（bimodality），这在顺序执行中不存在；推理阶段组件最适合用对数正态分布描述；泊松指数零模型在所有拓扑下均被明确拒绝。这些流量特征差异进一步传播到推理和网络层指标。

Conclusion: 经典的人驱动流量模型不适用于多智能体LLM系统，需要为这类新兴工作负载开发专门的流量建模方法。论文公开了框架和分析流水线（https://github.com/dlamagna/agentraffic）以支持后续研究和复现。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems are an emerging networked workload whose rapid deployment raises questions about the traffic patterns they generate. Compared to conventional applications, these systems generate requests internally: a single user task can induce a structured sequence of model calls whose timing is governed by coordination logic rather than by user arrival rate. It is not clear whether classical traffic models, designed for human-driven workloads, apply to this setting.
  We present an empirical characterisation of LLM-call interarrival time distributions across sequential, star, and full-mesh agentic coordination topologies, using a multi-layer measurement framework over 500 repeated runs per topology. We find that topology fundamentally shapes the arrival process of requests to the LLM backend: fan-out coordination introduces a structural bimodality absent in sequential execution, and the reasoningphase component is best described by a log-normal distribution, with the Poisson exponential null model decisively rejected across all topologies. These differences propagate to inference and network level metrics. The framework and analysis pipeline are released openly at https://github.com/dlamagna/agentraffic.

</details>


### [262] [Making Time-Sensitive Networking Deployable: A Comprehensive Lifecycle Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20500)
*Rubi Debnath,Paul Pop,Silviu S. Craciunas,Marc Boyer,Sebastian Steinhorst*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文对时间敏感网络（TSN）的部署生命周期进行了全面综述，指出现有研究多聚焦于单一目标优化（如可调度性、延迟、求解速度），但这些方法论的假设与实际硬件约束存在差异，导致其在实际系统中难以部署。文章总结了TSN部署的挑战、现有工具的局限性，并提出了未来研究方向，识别了从部署视角出发的关键研究空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的TSN研究大多关注单个优化目标（如可调度性、延迟、求解速度、性能分析），但这些研究往往基于理想化假设，与真实硬件约束不符。目前缺乏一个统一的、端到端的TSN部署工作流视角，也缺少协调一致的研究努力来弥合学术研究与实际部署之间的鸿沟。

Method: 本文采用综述方法，从端到端视角全面概述TSN部署生命周期，包括当前挑战、现有工具局限性以及未来研究方向，并基于部署视角识别关键研究空白。

Result: 文章呈现了TSN部署生命周期的全景图，指出了当前研究与实际部署之间的差距，识别了从部署视角出发的关键研究空白，并为下一代可部署TSN网络的开发提供了指导。

Conclusion: TSN的实际部署是一个复杂的多步骤工程任务，需要超越单一目标优化的整体视角。未来研究应聚焦于弥合假设与实际硬件约束之间的差距，推动统一、端到端的TSN部署和管理工作流的建立。

Abstract: Time-Sensitive Networking (TSN) provides deterministic guarantees and bounded latency for safety-critical applications across different domains. While the IEEE 802.1 TSN standards provide a comprehensive set of mechanisms for time synchronization, shaping, scheduling, resource reservation, and reliability, deploying TSN in real-world applications remains a complex, multi-step engineering task. Existing research primarily focuses on optimizing individual objectives, such as increasing schedulability, reducing latency, accelerating solution generation, or improving performance analysis. However, whether these extensively researched solutions can be deployed in real-world systems remains unanswered, as the assumptions made in these methodologies often differ from practical hardware constraints. Therefore, a broader, holistic view of end-to-end TSN deployment and management remains an open research question. Currently, there is a lack of a unified perspective, consensus, and coordinated research efforts focusing on a comprehensive end-to-end TSN deployment workflow. We present a comprehensive overview of the TSN deployment lifecycle, current challenges, limitations of existing tools, and future research directions for TSN deployment and management. Lastly, we identify key research gaps from a deployment perspective and provide guidance for the development of next-generation deployable TSN networks.

</details>


### [263] [Fluid-Dynamic Interference Modeling for LEO Mega-Constellations: A Spatiotemporal Kinetic Field Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20651)
*Wen-Yu Dong,Weiwei Jiang,Song Zhao,Rui-Si Han,Qi Bi,Sheng Chen*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种动力学干扰场框架，将低轨巨型星座建模为在外力运动学下演化的可压缩流体壳层，通过流体力学守恒律推导出时变中断概率的闭式表达式，并揭示了高纬度"干扰涌浪"源于轨道压缩和边界通量而非随机异常。数值验证表明该框架精确，且发现约79°倾角低轨设计比传统90°极轨在设计权衡上更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低轨巨型星座造成的干扰环境高度非平稳，传统静态随机几何快照方法无法准确刻画其时间演化特性。作者旨在建立能够捕捉卫星运动导致的干扰时空动态变化的分析工具，以支撑6G非地面网络的干扰感知设计。

Method: 将卫星星座类比为可压缩流体壳层，把卫星运动映射为连续通量场，推导聚合干扰的流体动力学守恒律；通过矩匹配方法获得时变中断概率的闭式表达式；并通过星历驱动的蒙特卡洛模拟进行数值验证。

Result: 框架在时间演化、纬度和设计参数维度上均与蒙特卡洛模拟高度吻合；揭示了高纬度干扰涌浪的流体动力学成因；发现约79°倾角、低轨道的设计相比传统90°极轨在中断概率、覆盖连续性与干扰隔离之间取得更优权衡。

Conclusion: 该动力学干扰场框架提供了可解析处理的工具，为6G非地面网络的干扰感知设计提供了新视角，挑战了极轨设计的普适最优性假设。

Abstract: Low Earth orbit (LEO) mega-constellations create a highly non-stationary interference environment that cannot be accurately captured by static stochastic-geometry snapshots. This paper proposes a kinetic interference field framework that models the constellation as a compressible fluid shell evolving under orbital kinematics. By mapping satellite motion into a continuum flux field, we derive a hydrodynamic conservation law for the aggregate interference and obtain a closed-form expression for the time-varying outage probability via moment matching. The analysis reveals that high-latitude ``interference surges'' are a direct consequence of orbital compression and boundary flux, rather than random anomalies. Numerical validation against ephemeris-driven Monte Carlo simulations confirms the accuracy of the framework across time evolution, latitude, and design parameters. Leveraging the closed-form model, we further show that the conventional $90^{\circ}$ polar-orbit design is not universally outage-optimal. Instead, an inclination angle near $79^{\circ}$ at low altitude achieves a favorable trade-off between coverage continuity and geometric interference isolation. The proposed framework provides a tractable analytical tool for interference-aware 6G non-terrestrial network (NTN) design.

</details>


### [264] [Mitigating Proxy-Induced Traffic Drift in Website Fingerprinting via Model-Agnostic Traffic Tailoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20692)
*Linxiao Yu,Tianyu Cui,Xinhao Deng,Yuqi Qing,Jun Tao,Ke Xu,Qi Li*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出PA3，一个模型无关的预处理框架，用于分析和缓解代理协议引起的特征漂移问题，从而提升网站指纹识别模型在未见代理协议流量上的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户常依赖多种代理协议绕过审查，而基于深度学习的网站指纹识别模型在一种协议上训练后，面对未见过的代理协议流量时性能严重下降。作者将这一问题归因于"代理诱导的特征漂移"——同一网站在不同代理协议下的流量模式存在差异，现有模型无法捕捉这种差异。

Method: PA3包含两个核心步骤：(1) 首先对协议特定的漂移进行指纹识别（fingerprinting）；(2) 利用这些指纹对代理流量进行定制化预处理以实现特征对齐（feature alignment），从而减轻漂移并提升WF模型在未见协议上的泛化能力。PA3是模型无关的，可适用于不同的WF模型。

Result: 大量实验表明，PA3在未见协议上平均提升F1分数0.12（约27%相对提升），不同模型上最高可获得0.41的绝对提升。在最佳情况下，PA3使WF模型在未见协议的流量上获得超过0.96的F1分数，显著缩小了漂移带来的性能差距。

Conclusion: PA3通过分析并缓解代理诱导的特征漂移，有效提升了网站指纹识别模型对未见代理协议的泛化能力，为多协议环境下WF的实际部署提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Website fingerprinting (WF) based on deep learning can effectively identify websites from encrypted traffic. However, users often rely on proxy protocols to bypass censorship, and the diversity of these protocols poses a major challenge, as WF models trained on traffic from one set of protocols perform poorly when evaluated on that from unseen protocols. We attribute this issue to proxy-induced feature drift, where traffic patterns of the same website vary with the proxy protocol, leading to discrepancies that WF models fail to capture and severe performance degradation. To tackle this issue, we propose PA3, a model-agnostic preprocessing framework to analyze and mitigate the proxy-induced drift. PA3 first fingerprints the protocol-specific drift. These fingerprints are then used to tailor the proxied traffic for feature alignment, which mitigates the drift and considerably improves the generalization of WF models on traffic from unseen protocols. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that PA3 substantially enhances generalization on unseen protocols with an average improvement of 0.12 in F1-score (roughly 27% relative), achieving up to a 0.41 absolute gain across models, which narrows the performance gap introduced by the drift. In the best case, PA3 enables WF models to obtain F1-scores above 0.96 on traffic from unseen protocols.

</details>


### [265] [Orchra: Stateful-aware Cross-slice Workload Migrations in the 6G Control Plane](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20893)
*Anthony Kiggundu,Bin Han,Hans D. Schotten*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出Orchra，一个用于5G-Advanced/6G网络中智能编排器，通过将用户设备关键状态（NAS上下文、安全密钥、PDU会话信息）外部化到临时暂存层，实现有状态、低延迟的切片间/切片内会话迁移，将用户面中断时间从传统3GPP方法的245.50ms降低超过一半，且安全开销可忽略不计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有云原生5G架构缺乏对切片间/切片内有状态会话迁移的原生支持，依赖高开销的NAS重注册和容器重新部署等方式，导致用户面流量中断时间长达245.50ms，无法满足5G-Advanced和6G网络对无缝运行时切片转换的实际需求。

Method: Orchra通过将关键的用户设备状态（包括NAS上下文、安全密钥和PDU会话信息）外部化到一个临时暂存层，从而在切片边界之间保持会话连续性，避免完整的重新注册过程，实现有状态的、低延迟的上下文传输。

Result: 实验评估表明，与传统3GPP方法相比，Orchra将用户面中断时间减少了两倍以上，同时安全开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: Orchra为云原生5G-Advanced网络中实现无缝、状态保持的切片转换提供了一种实用且可复现的方法，展示了其在6G网络中的潜在应用价值。

Abstract: Network slicing is a foundational capability of Fifth Generation (5G)-Advanced and emerging Sixth Generation (6G) networks, yet practical support for seamless runtime slice transitions remains limited. Standard cloud-native 5G architectures lack native support for stateful inter/intra-slice session migration, relying instead on high-overhead Non-Access Stratum (NAS) re-registrations, container redeployment etc., which disrupt userplane traffic for up to 245.50 ms. To address this limitation, we present Orchra, an intelligent orchestrator for stateful, low-latency context transfer. By externalizing critical user equipment state-including NAS context, security keys, and Protocol Data Unit (PDU) session information-into a transient staging layer, Orchra preserves session continuity across slice boundaries without requiring full re-registration.
  Experimental evaluation shows that Orchra reduces this userplane interruption by more than twice in comparison to conventional Third Generation Partnership Project (3GPP)-based approaches while incurring negligible security overhead. These results demonstrate a practical and reproducible approach for enabling seamless, state-preserving slice transitions in cloud-native 5G-Advanced networks.

</details>


<div id='cs.SI'></div>

# cs.SI [[Back]](#toc)

### [266] [Beyond Truth Discovery: A Two-Stage Framework to Assess the Severity of False Claim during Disasters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20983)
*Ruichen Yao,Tejna Dasari,Gulshat Baispay,Aizhan Zaurbek,Yifan Liu,Yaokun Liu,Zelin Li,Dong Wang*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个两阶段框架来评估灾难期间社交媒体虚假声明的严重程度。第一阶段利用多模态代理从包含文本、图像、视频和链接的帖子中提取虚假声明并进行验证；第二阶段将虚假声明的严重性定义为可信度和危害性两个维度的组合，并构建了基于Reddit飓风和野火帖子的声明级严重性基准。实验表明，传统监督模型与人类判断的对齐度有限，而LLM表现更强，其中上下文学习与人类判断的对齐效果最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注社交媒体帖子是否包含虚假信息，但对帖子中嵌入的具体虚假声明及其严重程度缺乏深入洞察。为弥补这一局限，需要更细粒度的虚假声明识别和严重性评估方法。

Method: 提出两阶段框架：第一阶段开发虚假声明提取代理，从多模态社交媒体帖子中识别虚假声明并通过证据验证；第二阶段将虚假声明严重性定义为可信度与危害性两个维度的结合，由人类标注者评估构建声明级基准，并将严重性评估视为人类-AI对齐问题，通过共享评估准则检验模型能否复现人类判断。

Result: 在构建的基准上，传统监督模型与人类判断的对齐有限，LLM显著更强；在各评估策略中，上下文学习（in-context learning）始终取得与人类判断最强的对齐效果。

Conclusion: 虚假声明严重性评估需要超越简单的二分类标签预测，应从人类-AI对齐的角度出发；LLM结合人类示例和共享决策标准是评估虚假声明严重性的有效途径。

Abstract: False information spreads rapidly on social media during disasters and can undermine emergency response efforts, public trust, and crisis communication. Existing research primarily focuses on determining whether social media posts contain false information, but provides limited insight into the specific false claims embedded within posts and the severity of individual false claims. To address the limitations, we propose a two-stage framework to assess the severity of false claims during disasters. In the first stage, we develop a false claim extraction agent that identifies false claims from multimodal social media posts containing text, images, videos, and links. A subsequent verification step validates extracted claims with supporting evidence. In the second stage, we define false claim severity as the combination of two complementary dimensions: believability, which determines the likelihood that a claim will be believed, and harmfulness, which captures the potential consequences if it is believed. Human annotators assess both dimensions to construct a claim-level severity benchmark using false claims extracted from Reddit posts related to hurricanes and wildfires. Building upon this benchmark, we investigate false claim severity assessment as a human-AI alignment problem, evaluating whether models can reproduce human judgments under a shared evaluation rubric rather than merely predicting severity labels. Experiments on the benchmark show that traditional supervised models exhibit limited alignment with human judgments, whereas Large Language Models (LLMs) achieve substantially stronger performance. Among the evaluated strategies, in-context learning consistently achieves the strongest alignment with human judgments, highlighting the importance of human examples and shared decision criteria for severity assessment.

</details>


### [267] [From Propagation to Protection: Risk-Aware Diffusion for Harm Minimization in Signed Social Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21040)
*Aaqib Zahoor,Janibul Bashir,Iqra Altaf Gillani*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文提出RASH，一种带符号、对易感性敏感的连续非单调扩散模型，支持意识反转（正面转负面），并在此基础上提出Harm Minimization（HM）目标，兼顾传播范围与危害最小化，在六个带符号网络上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型（如IC、LT、IM、Inf-Min）将激活视为离散且不可逆，无法建模信念减弱、反转或恢复；同时对个体统一对待，忽略易感性差异与高风险个体的保护需求。

Method: 提出RASH模型：节点意识连续、有界且非单调；证明仅含正/负边的情形下仍保持单调性和γ-弱次模性，保证贪心逼近。在RASH基础上定义Harm Minimization（HM），最大化总覆盖并最小化意识缺口（危害），证明NP-hard但保留单调与弱次模结构，支持有界近似比的贪心算法。

Result: 在六个结构多样的带符号网络中，RASH是所测试模型中唯一允许意识在激活后反转的模型；HM在所有评测方法（包括其边界情形IM和Inf-Min）中实现最高危害降低。

Conclusion: RASH与HM为带符号网络中的危害最小化提供了理论上可追踪、实际上有效的框架，突破了传统模型不可逆和忽略个体易感性的局限。

Abstract: Real-world social relationships are not uniformly supportive. Information through hostile connections can increase resistance, anxiety, or misinformation rather than adoption. Classical models such as Independent Cascade and Linear Threshold, together with Influence Maximization (IM), which maximizes spread from a limited seed set, treat activation as discrete and irreversible. Its counterpart, Influence Minimization (Inf-Min), limits undesirable spread but similarly relies on simplified activation assumptions. Signed extensions incorporate polarity but largely retain this irreversibility, leaving no room for beliefs to weaken, reverse, or recover under competing influence. Moreover, both objectives typically treat individuals uniformly, without accounting for differences in vulnerability or prioritizing protection of those most at risk. We introduce RASH, a signed, susceptibility-aware diffusion model in which node awareness is continuous, bounded, and non-monotonic, and prove that despite this added expressiveness it remains monotone and γ-weakly submodular where only positive or negative edges exist, preserving tractable greedy approximation guarantees where strict submodularity provably fails. Building on RASH, we formulate Harm Minimization (HM), which maximizes aggregate reach while minimizing the awareness shortfall (harm). We prove HM is NP-hard, yet its harm-reduction formulation inherits the same monotonicity and weak-submodularity structure, admitting a greedy algorithm with a bounded approximation ratio. Across six structurally diverse signed networks, RASH is the only diffusion model tested to our knowledge that ever allows awareness to reverse after activation, letting sustained discouraging influence drive awareness from positive toward negative, and HM achieves the highest harm reduction of any method evaluated, including its own boundary cases (IM and Inf-Min)

</details>
